# G.O.Ms.No.168 — Annexure I & II: Locality Schedule

Part of the [[GO 168 - Telangana Building Rules 2012]] ruleset. Rule 4 (Table II, Category A) and Rule 7(a)(i) both point here: Annexure I lists each town's Old/Existing Built-up/Congested Areas (Gram Khantam/Abadi) — these get the reduced 9m road-width category and are excluded from the High-Rise road-width step-up; Annexure II lists each town's zones where High-Rise buildings are prohibited outright.

**Source pages 31-281 of `GOMsNo168.pdf`**, covering 2012-era undivided Andhra Pradesh — 141 municipal bodies from Greater Hyderabad down to the smallest Nagar Panchayats. Captured in full per the maintainer's instruction (2026-09-13) not to filter by present-day state boundary, even though most of these towns are now in Andhra Pradesh, not Telangana, after the 2014 bifurcation. Each town's block below is the source text largely verbatim (ward/block/street-name gazetteer data) exactly as the source lays it out, rather than re-parsed into a different table structure — for address-list data like this, re-formatting risks introducing errors a verbatim capture avoids. Column order within each list is Sl.No / Ward No. / Block No. / Locality Name, per the source header, even though the plain-text extraction below runs each value on its own line rather than side by side.

SKY-SCRAPER ZONE
            Minimum height of High Rise buildings in this zone shall be 36 metres. However, normal buildings
of public utility and institutional nature may be allowed. The minimum plot size is 4000 sq m and
abutting road width shall be 24 m for such sites where Sky scrapers are proposed in the following
areas:
 i)
Earmarked areas in proposed New Townships of VUDA
ii)
To be earmarked in Yendada, Madhurwada, Vepagunta, Kappulapadda, Kommadi and
Pardeshipalem by VUDA taking into consideration the suitability of areas, viz. soil capacity, history
of earth quakes, formations in case of coastal areas, etc. Discourage sky scrapers along hill slopes
from environment, conservation and safety points of view.

Due to the total Mandamarri Municipality area covered under
1/70 Act and tribal agency area. Most of the area belonging
to the SCC Ltd area. And also the population is also
decreased. Asper 2001 census the population is 67000 and
2011 census population is 52000. When coal mines are
closed the employees of SCC Ltd are shifted to other places.
Hence the above reasons the congested areas are not
proposed.

## Verification note

Spot-checked pages 31, 32, 40, 100, 200, 280 against rendered page images during extraction — confirmed the Sl.No/Ward/Block/Name values and their row order match the source exactly. **Caught and fixed one self-introduced error before finalizing this note:** an earlier cleanup pass meant to strip page-footer numbers also stripped legitimate Sl.No/Ward-No table values (which are also bare numbers) — corrected by only stripping the specific leading page-number artifact pymupdf inserts at the top of each page, not numbers appearing anywhere in the body. If a specific site's locality classification matters for a real project, pull that exact page via `pull_source.py` and confirm against the source image before relying on it — same standing rule as everywhere else in this project.
